TDN.AccessControl.absent_path_is_denied (roles : String → Option String)
  (permissions : List TDN.AccessControl.Permission) (request : TDN.AccessControl.Request)
  (excluded : ∀ permission ∈ permissions, permission.path ≠ request.normalizedPath) :
  TDN.AccessControl.authorize roles permissions request = false
TDN.AccessControl.role_permission_restricts_operations (roles : String → Option String)
  (permissions : List TDN.AccessControl.Permission) (request : TDN.AccessControl.Request) (role : String)
  (bound : roles request.authenticatedFingerprint = some role)
  (allowed : TDN.AccessControl.authorize roles permissions request = true) (permitted : String → String → Prop)
  (checked : ∀ permission ∈ permissions, permission.role = role → permitted permission.method permission.path) :
  permitted request.method request.normalizedPath
TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.accepted_messages_from_seeded_history {Key Producer Header Wire Annotation Plain : Type}
  [DecidableEq Header] (produces : TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.Message Key Producer Wire Annotation → Prop)
  (authenticates :
    List (TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.Message Key Producer Wire Annotation) →
      TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.Decoded Key Wire Plain → Prop)
  (compatible : Key → Key → Prop) (project : Annotation → Plain)
  (sound : TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.AuthenticationSound compatible project authenticates)
  (property : TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.Message Key Producer Wire Annotation → Prop)
  (publication :
    ∀ (message : TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.Message Key Producer Wire Annotation), produces message → property message)
  {before after : TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.State Key Producer Header Wire Annotation Plain}
  {actions : List TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.Action}
  (initial : TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.Safe compatible project property before)
  (run : (TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.system produces authenticates).MTr before actions after)
  (decoded : TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.Decoded Key Wire Plain) :
  decoded ∈ after.accepted →
    (∃ message ∈ after.published, TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.Matches compatible project message decoded) ∧
      ∀ message ∈ after.published,
        TDN.AuthenticatedAssembly.Matches compatible project message decoded → property message

TDN.Execution.inbound_crossing_requires_local_decryption (c : TDN.Execution.Config) (layout : ℕ → List ℕ)
  (tag component : ℕ) (conditions : TDN.Execution.LocalStackConditions c layout)
  (inboundTags : ∀ (port : ℕ), c.owner port = component → ∀ a ∈ c.inbound port, a.tag = tag)
  {before after : TDN.Execution.State} {actions : List TDN.Execution.Action}
  (run :
    (TDN.RestrictedExecution.within (TDN.Execution.lts c) fun s => c.owner s.port = component).MTr before actions after)
  (received : before.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.received) (unauthenticated : before.authenticated = [])
  (outside : tag ∈ layout before.port) (ready : after.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.ready)
  (inside : tag ∉ layout after.port) :
  TDN.TraceEvents.Contains (TDN.Execution.lts c) before actions after fun left action right =>
    action = TDN.Execution.Action.decrypt ∧
      left.frame.tags = tag :: right.frame.tags ∧
        left.frame.payload = before.frame.payload ∧ c.owner left.port = component

  TDN.Execution.DomainInvariant c layout allowed (TDN.Execution.withHiddenContent payload domain envelopes after)
TDN.Filter.allowed_has_matching_allow {Packet : Type} (rules : List (TDN.Filter.Rule Packet)) (p : Packet)
  (h : TDN.Filter.evaluate rules TDN.Filter.Verdict.deny p = TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow) :
  ∃ r ∈ rules, r.accepts p = true ∧ r.verdict = TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow
TDN.Filter.default_deny_sound {Packet : Type} (rules : List (TDN.Filter.Rule Packet)) (permitted : Packet → Prop)
  (localRule : ∀ r ∈ rules, r.verdict = TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow → ∀ (p : Packet), r.accepts p = true → permitted p)
  (p : Packet) (accepted : TDN.Filter.evaluate rules TDN.Filter.Verdict.deny p = TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow) : permitted p
TDN.Filter.rejects_outside_allowlist {Packet : Type} (rules : List (TDN.Filter.Rule Packet)) (permitted : Packet → Prop)
  (localRule : ∀ r ∈ rules, r.verdict = TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow → ∀ (p : Packet), r.accepts p = true → permitted p)
  (p : Packet) (forbidden : ¬permitted p) :
  TDN.Filter.evaluate rules TDN.Filter.Verdict.deny p = TDN.Filter.Verdict.deny

TDN.HistoricalResponses.all_historical_responses_preserve_safety {S A : Type} (network : Cslib.LTS S A)
  (config : TDN.KernelResponses.Config S) (safe : S → Prop) (preserves : network.TrInv safe)
  (replyPreserves :
    ∀ (source : S) (header : TDN.PacketPolicy.Packet) (port : ℕ),
      safe source →
        config.generates source →
          TDN.KernelResponses.ResponseProtocol header.protocol →
            config.selectedRoute source header port →
              TDN.Filter.evaluate (List.map TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule.toFilter (config.outputRules source port))
                    (config.outputFallback source port) (TDN.KernelResponses.outputPacket config header port) =
                  TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow →
                config.interfaceName port ≠ "lo" → safe (config.emit source header port))
  {before after : TDN.HistoricalResponses.State S}
  {actions : List (TDN.KernelResponses.Action (TDN.HistoricalResponses.Action A))}
  (run :
    (TDN.KernelResponses.lts (TDN.HistoricalResponses.lts network) (TDN.HistoricalResponses.responseConfig config)).MTr
      (TDN.KernelResponses.State.network before) actions (TDN.KernelResponses.State.network after))
  (initial : TDN.HistoricalResponses.Safe safe before) : TDN.HistoricalResponses.Safe safe after
TDN.Imported.Authentication.observed_capabilities_distinguish_request_and_response :
  TDN.Imported.Authentication.advertisedCapabilities ≠ [] ∧
    ∀ a ∈ TDN.Imported.Authentication.advertisedCapabilities, a.2 = if a.1 = true then [2, 3, 4, 5] else [3]
TDN.Imported.Authentication.observed_credentials_and_configured_hash_constraints :
  TDN.Imported.Authentication.credentials ≠ [] ∧
    ∀ c ∈ TDN.Imported.Authentication.credentials,
      c.2.1 = "secp384r1" ∧ c.2.2.1 = "ecdsa-384-sha384" ∧ c.2.2.2 = "ecdsa-384-sha384"
TDN.Imported.Authentication.recorded_sha256_refusal_and_sha384_recovery :
  TDN.Imported.Authentication.trial.1 = "ECDSA_WITH_SHA256_DER" ∧
    TDN.Imported.Authentication.trial.2.1 = "signature scheme not acceptable" ∧
      TDN.Imported.Authentication.trial.2.2.1 = "ECDSA_WITH_SHA384_DER" ∧ 0 < TDN.Imported.Authentication.trial.2.2.2
TDN.Imported.Authentication.selected_authentication_uses_sha384 :
  TDN.Imported.Authentication.selectedSignatures ≠ [] ∧
    ∀ s ∈ TDN.Imported.Authentication.selectedSignatures, s.1 = "ECDSA_WITH_SHA384_DER" ∧ s.2 = "ECDSA_WITH_SHA384_DER"

TDN.Imported.Family444V13.source_output_reply_layers {guard : ℕ × List TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule}
  (guardMember : guard ∈ TDN.Imported.Family444V13.responseSourceGuards) {entry output : TDN.Execution.PortData}
  (entryMember : entry ∈ TDN.Imported.Family444V13.ports) (entryOwner : entry.owner = guard.1)
  (inputExternal : entry.name ≠ "lo") (outputMember : output ∈ TDN.Imported.Family444V13.ports)
  (owner : output.owner = entry.owner) (outputExternal : output.name ≠ "lo") {auth : List ℕ}
  (authenticated :
    auth ∈
      TDN.Execution.possibleAuthentications (TDN.Execution.deviceAt TDN.Imported.Family444V13.devices entry.owner)
        entry)
  (original reply : TDN.PacketPolicy.Packet) (inputName : original.ingress = entry.name)
  (outputName : reply.egress = output.name) (inputAuth : original.inboundPolicies = auth)
  (selectorBound :
    ∀ id ∈ auth,
      ∃ association ∈ (TDN.Execution.deviceAt TDN.Imported.Family444V13.devices entry.owner).inbound,
        association.id = id ∧ association.matches original = true)
  (address : TDN.SourceResponseGuards.ValidSourceAddress original.source)
  (inputAccepted :
    TDN.Filter.evaluate (List.map TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule.toFilter guard.2) TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow original =
      TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow)
  (outputAccepted :
    TDN.Filter.evaluate
        (List.map TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule.toFilter
          (TDN.Execution.deviceAt TDN.Imported.Family444V13.devices output.owner).outputRules)
        TDN.Filter.Verdict.deny reply =
      TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow)
  (returnsToSource : reply.destination = original.source)
  (protocol : TDN.KernelResponses.ResponseProtocol reply.protocol) :
  (if auth = [] then entry.stack else entry.stack.tail) = output.stack

  (run :

          (TDN.Targets.MixedByteOrigin TDN.Imported.PairingMacsecIpsecStandalone.redSources)).MTr

            labels packet.state

TDN.Imported.SameLevelBinding.observed_inner_peers_have_same_full_declared_level :
  TDN.Imported.SameLevelBinding.peerLevels ≠ [] ∧ ∀ p ∈ TDN.Imported.SameLevelBinding.peerLevels, p.1 = p.2
TDN.Imported.SameLevelBinding.positive_and_negative_witnesses :
  (∃ p ∈ TDN.Imported.SameLevelBinding.logicalProbes, p.2.2 = "delivered") ∧
    ∃ p ∈ TDN.Imported.SameLevelBinding.logicalProbes, p.1 ≠ p.2.1 ∧ p.2.2 = "no_reply"
TDN.Imported.SameLevelBinding.recorded_logical_domain_outcomes (p : String × String × String) :
  p ∈ TDN.Imported.SameLevelBinding.logicalProbes → p.2.2 = if p.1 = p.2.1 then "delivered" else "no_reply"
TDN.Imported.Supplemental.observed_good_crl_authentication_exchanges :
  TDN.Evidence.RecordedCertificateExchanges TDN.Imported.Supplemental.caExchanges
TDN.Imported.Supplemental.observed_ntp_replies_without_clock_adjustment :

  TDN.Imported.Supplemental.ospfCounts ≠ [] ∧ ∀ s ∈ TDN.Imported.Supplemental.ospfCounts, 0 < s.2.2.1 ∧ 0 < s.2.2.2
TDN.Imported.Supplemental.recorded_probe_expectations :
  TDN.Imported.Supplemental.probes ≠ [] ∧ ∀ p ∈ TDN.Imported.Supplemental.probes, p.expected = p.observed
TDN.Imported.Supplemental.recorded_relay_both_branches :
  TDN.Imported.Supplemental.relayCounts ≠ [] ∧
    ∀ s ∈ TDN.Imported.Supplemental.relayCounts, 0 < s.1 ∧ 0 < s.2.1 ∧ s.2.2 = s.1

  TDN.Evidence.TLSRecordedOutcomes TDN.Imported.Supplemental.tlsSamples
TDN.Imported.Supplemental.useful_and_negative_probe_witnesses :
  (∃ p ∈ TDN.Imported.Supplemental.probes, 0 < p.received) ∧
    ∃ p ∈ TDN.Imported.Supplemental.probes, p.observed = "no_reply"

TDN.KeySources.executions_have_only_authenticated_sources {config : TDN.KeySources.Config}
  (disabled : config.preSharedEnabled = false) {actions : List TDN.KeySources.Action} {state : TDN.KeySources.State}
  (execution : (TDN.KeySources.system config).MTr [] actions state) : TDN.KeySources.OnlyAuthenticatedSources state
TDN.LinkProtection.every_emitted_frame_is_encrypted {Body : Type} (config : TDN.LinkProtection.Config)
  (protect : config.protectFrames = true) (encrypt : config.encryptFrames = true)
  (zero : config.confidentialityOffset = 0) (sa : Option ℕ) (body : Body) (wire : TDN.LinkProtection.WireFrame Body)
  (emitted : TDN.LinkProtection.transmit config sa body = some wire) : TDN.LinkProtection.FullyEncrypted wire
TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.all_executions_preserve_original_ethernet (c : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Config)
  {before after : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.State} {actions : List TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Action}
  (run : (TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.lts c).MTr before actions after) :
  after.frame.body.originalEthernet = before.frame.body.originalEthernet
TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.all_executions_preserve_stack (c : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Config) (layout : ℕ → List ℕ)
  (conditions : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.LocalConditions c layout) {start finish : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.State}
  {actions : List TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Action} (run : (TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.lts c).MTr start actions finish)
  (initial : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.StackInvariant c layout start) :
  TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.StackInvariant c layout finish
TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.boundary_removal_has_actual_decryption (c : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Config) (tag : ℕ)
  {before after : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.State} {actions : List TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Action}
  (run : (TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.lts c).MTr before actions after)
  (initial : tag ∈ before.frame.body.protectionLayers) (final : tag ∉ after.frame.body.protectionLayers) :
  TDN.TraceEvents.Contains (TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.lts c) before actions after fun left action right =>
    TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.IsDecryption action ∧
      left.frame.body.protectionLayers = tag :: right.frame.body.protectionLayers ∧
        left.frame.payload = before.frame.payload
TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.inbound_crossing_has_actual_boundary_decryption (c : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Config)
  (layout : ℕ → List ℕ) (tag component : ℕ) (conditions : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.LocalConditions c layout)
  (carrierCheck : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.CarrierInputCheck c layout tag)
  (ipTags : ∀ (entry : ℕ), c.ip.owner entry = component → ∀ policy ∈ c.ip.inbound entry, policy.tag = tag)
  (macTags :
    ∀ port ∈ c.macPorts,
      c.ip.owner port.physicalPort = component → ∀ association ∈ port.receiveAssociations, association.tag = tag)
  {before after : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.State} {actions : List TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Action}
  (run :
    (TDN.RestrictedExecution.within (TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.lts c) fun state =>
          c.ip.owner state.port = component).MTr
      before actions after)
  (received : before.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.received) (empty : before.authenticated = [])
  (outside : tag ∈ layout before.port) (ready : after.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.ready)
  (inside : tag ∉ layout after.port) :
  TDN.TraceEvents.Contains (TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.lts c) before actions after fun left action right =>
    TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.BoundaryDecryption c tag left action right ∧
      c.ip.owner left.port = component ∧ left.frame.payload = before.frame.payload
TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.nested_stack_requires_ordered_encryptions (c : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Config)
  (layout : ℕ → List ℕ) (conditions : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.LocalConditions c layout) (innerTag outerTag : ℕ)
  (placement : ∀ (port : ℕ), (layout port).head? = some outerTag → layout port = [outerTag, innerTag])
  {before after : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.State} {actions : List TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Action}
  (run : (TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.lts c).MTr before actions after)
  (initial : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.StackInvariant c layout before)
  (empty : before.frame.body.protectionLayers = []) (present : outerTag ∈ after.frame.body.protectionLayers) :
  TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.OrderedEncryptions c innerTag outerTag before actions after
TDN.PacketPolicy.accepted_immediate_header (rules : List TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule) (allowed : List ℕ)
  (checked : ∀ r ∈ rules, r.verdict = TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow → TDN.PacketPolicy.ImmediateHeaderGuard allowed r)
  (p : TDN.PacketPolicy.Packet) (accepted : TDN.PacketPolicy.Accepts rules p) : p.ipv6NextHeader ∈ allowed
TDN.PacketPolicy.forwarding_policy_protects (rules : List TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule) (inside outside : String)
  (checked : ∀ r ∈ rules, r.verdict = TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow → TDN.PacketPolicy.Guarded r inside outside)
  (p : TDN.PacketPolicy.Packet)
  (accepted :
    TDN.Filter.evaluate (List.map TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule.toFilter rules) TDN.Filter.Verdict.deny p =
      TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow) :
  TDN.PacketPolicy.ProtectedForward inside outside p
TDN.Paths.no_firewall_free_crossing {Node Side : Type} (edge : Node → Node → Prop) (firewall : Node → Prop)
  (side : Node → Side) (localRule : ∀ (a b : Node), edge a b → ¬firewall a → ¬firewall b → side a = side b) {a b : Node}
  (start : ¬firewall a) (different : side a ≠ side b) : ¬Relation.ReflTransGen (TDN.Paths.Avoiding edge firewall) a b

TDN.SourceResponseGuards.finite_reply_checks_preserve_layers (devices : List TDN.Execution.DeviceData)
  (ports : List TDN.Execution.PortData) (guards : List (ℕ × List TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule))
  (checked : TDN.SourceResponseGuards.FiniteReplyChecks devices ports guards) {guard : ℕ × List TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule}
  (guardMember : guard ∈ guards) {entry output : TDN.Execution.PortData} (entryMember : entry ∈ ports)
  (entryOwner : entry.owner = guard.1) (inputExternal : entry.name ≠ "lo") (outputMember : output ∈ ports)
  (owner : output.owner = entry.owner) (outputExternal : output.name ≠ "lo") {auth : List ℕ}
  (authenticated : auth ∈ TDN.Execution.possibleAuthentications (TDN.Execution.deviceAt devices entry.owner) entry)
  (original reply : TDN.PacketPolicy.Packet) (inputName : original.ingress = entry.name)
  (outputName : reply.egress = output.name) (inputAuth : original.inboundPolicies = auth)
  (selectorBound :
    ∀ id ∈ auth,
      ∃ association ∈ (TDN.Execution.deviceAt devices entry.owner).inbound,
        association.id = id ∧ association.matches original = true)
  (covered : ∃ rule ∈ guard.2, rule.toFilter.accepts original = true)
  (inputAccepted :
    TDN.Filter.evaluate (List.map TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule.toFilter guard.2) TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow original =
      TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow)
  (outputAccepted :
    TDN.Filter.evaluate
        (List.map TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule.toFilter (TDN.Execution.deviceAt devices output.owner).outputRules)
        TDN.Filter.Verdict.deny reply =
      TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow)
  (returnsToSource : reply.destination = original.source)
  (protocol : TDN.KernelResponses.ResponseProtocol reply.protocol) :
  (if auth = [] then entry.stack else entry.stack.tail) = output.stack

TDN.Targets.assembled_packet_at_black_keeps_each_byte_protected {Byte : Type} (c : TDN.Execution.Config)
  (sources domains blackPorts : List ℕ) (layout : ℕ → ℕ → List ℕ) (allowed : ℕ → ℕ → Prop) (innerTag : ℕ → ℕ)
  (outerTag : ℕ) (conditions : ∀ d ∈ domains, TDN.Execution.LocalDomainConditions c (layout d) (allowed d))
  (sourceCoverage : ∀ port ∈ sources, ∀ (d : ℕ), c.recipientDomain port = some d → d ∈ domains)
  (sourceChecks :
    ∀ d ∈ domains,
      ∀ port ∈ sources, c.recipientDomain port = some d → layout d port = [] ∧ c.payloadOrigin port ∧ allowed d port)
  (blackChecks : ∀ d ∈ domains, ∀ port ∈ blackPorts, layout d port = [outerTag, innerTag d])
  {after : List (TDN.ByteFlows.Carrier TDN.Execution.State (TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.ByteOrigin Byte ℕ ℕ))}
  {actions : List (TDN.ByteFlows.Action TDN.Execution.Action)}
  (run :
    (TDN.ByteFlows.lts (TDN.Execution.lts c) (TDN.Targets.RedByteOrigin c sources) TDN.Execution.PlaintextAssembly).MTr
      [] actions after)
  (packet : TDN.ByteFlows.Carrier TDN.Execution.State (TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.ByteOrigin Byte ℕ ℕ))
  (present : packet ∈ after) (ready : packet.state.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.ready)
  (black : packet.state.port ∈ blackPorts) (byte : TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.ByteOrigin Byte ℕ ℕ) :
  byte ∈ packet.bytes → packet.state.frame.tags = [outerTag, innerTag byte.domain]

  (protectedFrame : MACsecFrame) {finish : TDN.Encapsulation.State MACsecFrame}
  {actions : List TDN.Encapsulation.Action}
  (run :
    (TDN.Encapsulation.lts edge).MTr
      { node := source, frame := TDN.Encapsulation.Frame.wrapped destination protectedFrame } actions finish) :
  finish.frame = TDN.Encapsulation.Frame.wrapped destination protectedFrame ∨
    finish.node = destination ∧ finish.frame = TDN.Encapsulation.Frame.exposed protectedFrame

  (sources domains strict uniformOwners blackPorts : List ℕ) (layout : ℕ → ℕ → List ℕ) (allowed : ℕ → ℕ → Prop)
  (conditions : ∀ d ∈ domains, TDN.Execution.LocalDomainConditions c (layout d) (allowed d))
  (sourceCoverage : ∀ port ∈ sources, ∀ (d : ℕ), c.recipientDomain port = some d → d ∈ domains)
  (sourceChecks :
    ∀ d ∈ domains,
      ∀ port ∈ sources, c.recipientDomain port = some d → layout d port = [] ∧ c.payloadOrigin port ∧ allowed d port)
  (guards : TDN.Targets.GatewayResponseGuards c strict)
  (uniform : ∀ d ∈ domains, ∀ owner ∈ uniformOwners, TDN.UniformDomainResponses.Uniform c (layout d) (allowed d) owner)
  (innerTag : ℕ → ℕ) (outerTag : ℕ)
  (blackChecks : ∀ d ∈ domains, ∀ port ∈ blackPorts, layout d port = [outerTag, innerTag d])
  (placement :
    ∀ d ∈ domains, ∀ (port : ℕ), (layout d port).head? = some outerTag → layout d port = [outerTag, innerTag d])
  {after :
    List
      (TDN.ByteFlows.Carrier (TDN.KernelResponses.State TDN.Execution.State)
        (TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.ByteOrigin Byte ℕ ℕ))}
  {actions : List (TDN.ByteFlows.Action (TDN.KernelResponses.Action TDN.Execution.Action))}
  (run :
    (TDN.ResponseByteFlows.system (TDN.Execution.lts c)
          (TDN.UniformDomainResponses.responseConfig c strict uniformOwners) (TDN.Targets.RedByteOrigin c sources)
          TDN.Execution.PlaintextAssembly).MTr
      [] actions after)
  (finish : TDN.Execution.State) (bytes : List (TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.ByteOrigin Byte ℕ ℕ))
  (packetPresent : { state := TDN.KernelResponses.State.network finish, bytes := bytes } ∈ after)
  (ready : finish.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.ready) (black : finish.port ∈ blackPorts)
  (byte : TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.ByteOrigin Byte ℕ ℕ) (bytePresent : byte ∈ bytes) :
  ∃ start labels,
    TDN.Targets.RedByteOrigin c sources start byte ∧
      TDN.Targets.BytePathOrderedEncryptions c strict uniformOwners (innerTag byte.domain) outerTag
        (TDN.KernelResponses.State.network start) labels (TDN.KernelResponses.State.network finish)
TDN.Targets.every_black_fragment_has_both_layers {Byte : Type} (c : TDN.Execution.Config) (layout : ℕ → List ℕ)
  (sources blackPorts : List ℕ) (encodedBytes : TDN.Execution.Frame → List Byte)
  (conditions : TDN.Execution.LocalStackConditions c layout)
  (sourceCheck : ∀ port ∈ sources, layout port = [] ∧ c.payloadOrigin port)
  (blackCheck : ∀ port ∈ blackPorts, layout port = [2, 1])
  {start finish : TDN.Fragmentation.SegmentedState TDN.Execution.State TDN.Execution.FragmentDescriptor Byte}
  {actions : List (Option TDN.Execution.Action)} (origin : TDN.Targets.RedPayloadOrigin c sources start.logical)
  (whole : start.fragments = none)
  (run :
    (TDN.Fragmentation.segmented (TDN.Execution.lts c) (TDN.Execution.encodeForFragmentation encodedBytes)).MTr start
      actions finish)
  (ready : finish.logical.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.ready) (black : finish.logical.port ∈ blackPorts)
  (pieces : List (TDN.Fragmentation.Fragment TDN.Execution.FragmentDescriptor Byte)) :
  finish.fragments = some pieces →
    ∀ piece ∈ pieces,
      piece.header.layers = [2, 1] ∧
        piece.header.payload = finish.logical.frame.payload ∧
          piece.header.originDomain = finish.logical.frame.originDomain ∧
            piece.header.source = finish.logical.frame.packet.source ∧
              piece.header.destination = finish.logical.frame.packet.destination
TDN.Targets.every_mixed_black_byte_has_ordered_encryptions {Byte : Type} (c : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Config)
  (layout : ℕ → List ℕ) (sources blackPorts : List ℕ) (conditions : TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.LocalConditions c layout)
  (sourceChecks : ∀ port ∈ sources, layout port = [] ∧ c.ip.payloadOrigin port)
  (blackChecks : ∀ port ∈ blackPorts, layout port = [2, 1])
  (placement : ∀ (port : ℕ), (layout port).head? = some 2 → layout port = [2, 1])
  {after : List (TDN.ByteFlows.Carrier TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.State Byte)}
  {actions : List (TDN.ByteFlows.Action TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.Action)}
  (run : (TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.byteSystem c (TDN.Targets.MixedByteOrigin sources)).MTr [] actions after)
  (packet : TDN.ByteFlows.Carrier TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.State Byte) (member : packet ∈ after)
  (ready : packet.state.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.ready) (black : packet.state.port ∈ blackPorts) (byte : Byte)
  (contains : byte ∈ packet.bytes) :
  packet.state.frame.body.protectionLayers = [2, 1] ∧
    ∃ start labels,
      TDN.Targets.MixedByteOrigin sources start byte ∧
        (TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.byteLineage c).MTr start labels packet.state ∧
          TDN.MultiProtocolExecution.ByteOrderedEncryptions c 1 2 start labels packet.state
TDN.Targets.generated_administration_matrix_covers (components : List ℕ) (person : ℕ)
  (done : ℕ → TDN.Targets.AdministratorDuty → Bool) (component : ℕ) :
  component ∈ components →
    ∀ duty ∈ TDN.Targets.CoreAdministratorDuties,
      ∃
        record ∈
          List.flatMap
            (fun component =>
              List.map
                (fun duty =>
                  { administrator := person, component := component, duty := duty, completed := done component duty })
                TDN.Targets.CoreAdministratorDuties)
            components,
        record.component = component ∧ record.duty = duty
TDN.Targets.generated_audits_cover_annual_windows (windows : List TDN.Targets.AuditWindow)
  (build : TDN.Targets.AuditWindow → TDN.Targets.ComplianceAudit) (latestVersionAt : ℕ → String)
  (checked :
    ∀ w ∈ windows,
      (build w).window = w.id ∧
        w.start ≤ (build w).performedAt ∧
          (build w).performedAt ≤ w.finish ∧
            (build w).cpVersion = latestVersionAt (build w).performedAt ∧
              (build w).deliveredToAO = true ∧ (build w).reviewedByAO = true)
  (window : TDN.Targets.AuditWindow) :
  window ∈ windows →
    ∃ audit ∈ List.map build windows,
      audit.window = window.id ∧
        window.start ≤ audit.performedAt ∧
          audit.performedAt ≤ window.finish ∧
            audit.cpVersion = latestVersionAt audit.performedAt ∧ audit.deliveredToAO = true ∧ audit.reviewedByAO = true
TDN.Targets.generated_baselines_cover_components (components : List ℕ) (build : ℕ → TDN.Targets.BaselineRecord)
  (identity : ∀ c ∈ components, (build c).component = c) :
  TDN.RecordCoverage.Covers TDN.Targets.BaselineRecord.component components (List.map build components)
TDN.Targets.generated_reviews_cover_actions_and_events (actions events : List ℕ)
  (review : ℕ → TDN.Targets.SecurityReview) (identity : ∀ event ∈ actions ++ events, (review event).event = event) :
  TDN.RecordCoverage.Covers TDN.Targets.SecurityReview.event (actions ++ events) (List.map review (actions ++ events))
TDN.Targets.generated_siem_rules_cover_required_events (endpoints : List TDN.Targets.SIEMEndpoint)
  (requiredEvents : ℕ → List String) (endpoint : TDN.Targets.SIEMEndpoint) :
  endpoint ∈ endpoints →
    ∀ event ∈ requiredEvents endpoint.id,
      ∃
        rule ∈
          List.flatMap
            (fun endpoint =>
              List.map (fun event => { siem := endpoint.id, eventKind := event, enabled := true })
                (requiredEvents endpoint.id))
            endpoints,
        rule.siem = endpoint.id ∧ rule.eventKind = event ∧ rule.enabled = true
TDN.Targets.generated_test_plan_covers_obligations (obligations : List TDN.Targets.TestObligation)
  (execute : TDN.Targets.TestObligation → TDN.Targets.TestExecution)
  (identity : ∀ test ∈ obligations, (execute test).obligation = test.id)
  (retained : ∀ test ∈ obligations, (execute test).resultArtifact ≠ "") (obligation : TDN.Targets.TestObligation) :
  obligation ∈ obligations →
    obligation.id ∈ List.map TDN.Targets.TestObligation.id obligations ∧
      ∃ execution ∈ List.map execute obligations, execution.obligation = obligation.id ∧ execution.resultArtifact ≠ ""
TDN.Targets.gray_addressing_of_separated_ranges_and_named_rules (ranges : List (TDN.Addressing.Range TDN.SecurityLevel))
  (pairs : List TDN.Targets.IntendedAddressPair) (policies : List (List TDN.PacketPolicy.Rule))
  (valid : ∀ range ∈ ranges, range.low ≤ range.high) (separated : TDN.Addressing.Separated ranges)
  (peers :
    ∀ pair ∈ pairs,
      TDN.Addressing.Owns ranges pair.source pair.level ∧ TDN.Addressing.Owns ranges pair.destination pair.level)
  (rulesChecked :
    ∀ rules ∈ policies,
      ∀ rule ∈ rules, rule.verdict = TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow → TDN.Targets.RuleNamesPair pairs rule) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_gray_addressing_for_level_filtering ranges pairs policies
TDN.Targets.gray_firewall_paths_from_local_separation {Node Label : Type} (grayEdge : Node → Node → Prop)
  (grayFirewall inner management : Node → Prop) (level : Node → Label) (highest : Label)
  (dominates : Label → Label → Prop) (filtersIntendedSameLevel physicallyProtected : Node → Prop)
  (edgesChecked : ∀ (a b : Node), grayEdge a b → ¬grayFirewall a → ¬grayFirewall b → level a = level b)
  (innerNotFirewall : ∀ (component : Node), inner component → ¬grayFirewall component)
  (managementNotFirewall : ∀ (administrator : Node), management administrator → ¬grayFirewall administrator)
  (managementLevel : ∀ (administrator : Node), management administrator → level administrator = highest)
  (highestLevel : ∀ (component : Node), inner component → dominates highest (level component))
  (firewallChecks :
    ∀ (firewall : Node), grayFirewall firewall → filtersIntendedSameLevel firewall ∧ physicallyProtected firewall)
  (firewallPresent : (∃ a b, inner a ∧ inner b ∧ level a ≠ level b) → ∃ firewall, grayFirewall firewall) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_gray_firewall_path_coverage grayEdge grayFirewall inner management level highest dominates
    filtersIntendedSameLevel physicallyProtected
TDN.Targets.gray_firewall_policy_from_local_rules {P I : Type} (required : Bool) (policy : TDN.Targets.GrayPolicy P I)
  (safe :
    required = true →
      ∀ interface ∈ policy.interfaces,
        ∀ rule ∈ policy.rules interface,
          rule.verdict = TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow →
            ∀ (packet : P),
              rule.accepts packet = true →
                TDN.Targets.GrayCommonPermitted policy interface packet ∧
                  (policy.management packet → policy.managementPhysicalPort interface))
  (complete :
    required = true →
      ∀ interface ∈ policy.interfaces,
        ∀ (packet : P),
          policy.requiredTraffic interface packet → TDN.Targets.FirstAllowWitness (policy.rules interface) packet) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_gray_firewall_allowlist required policy
TDN.Targets.incident_reporting_from_generated_reports (incidents : List TDN.Targets.Incident) (registration : String)
  (primary alternate : TDN.Targets.IncidentContact) (product : ℕ → TDN.Targets.AffectedProduct)
  (delay : TDN.Targets.Incident → ℕ) (channel : TDN.Targets.IncidentChannel) (support : Bool)
  (required followed : List String) (registrationPresent : registration ≠ "")
  (primaryComplete : TDN.Targets.CompleteContact primary) (alternateComplete : TDN.Targets.CompleteContact alternate)
  (details :
    ∀ incident ∈ incidents,
      TDN.Targets.Reportable incident →
        incident.networkNames ≠ [] ∧ incident.description ≠ "" ∧ incident.remediation ≠ "")
  (products :
    ∀ incident ∈ incidents,
      TDN.Targets.Reportable incident →
        ∀ component ∈ incident.componentIds,
          (product component).component = component ∧ TDN.Targets.CompleteProduct (product component))
  (bounded : ∀ incident ∈ incidents, TDN.Targets.Reportable incident → delay incident ≤ 86400)
  (procedures : ∀ procedure ∈ required, procedure ∈ followed) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_incident_reporting incidents
    (List.map
      (fun incident =>
        { incident := incident, registrationNumber := registration, primary := primary, alternate := alternate,
          products := List.map product incident.componentIds, sentAt := incident.detectedAt + delay incident,
          channel := channel, nsaTechnicalSupportRequested := support })
      incidents)
    required followed

TDN.Targets.installed_certificate_has_component_key_generation (events actions : List TDN.Targets.ProvisioningEvent)
  (run :
    (TDN.Admission.lts TDN.Targets.CertificateProvisioningTrigger TDN.Targets.CertificateProvisioningPredecessor).MTr []
      actions events)
  (installation : TDN.Targets.ProvisioningEvent) (member : installation ∈ events)
  (kind : installation.kind = TDN.Targets.ProvisioningKind.installCertificate) :
  ∃ generation ∈ events,
    generation.kind = TDN.Targets.ProvisioningKind.generateKeyPair ∧
      generation.component = installation.component ∧
        generation.publicKey = installation.publicKey ∧
          generation.order < installation.order ∧
            generation.executor = TDN.Targets.ProvisioningExecutor.component installation.component

TDN.Targets.os_separation_from_role_family_sets (components : List TDN.Targets.Component)
  (innerFamilies outerFamilies : List String)
  (innerBinding :
    ∀ component ∈ components, component.role = TDN.Targets.ComponentRole.inner → component.osFamily ∈ innerFamilies)
  (outerBinding :
    ∀ component ∈ components, component.role = TDN.Targets.ComponentRole.outer → component.osFamily ∈ outerFamilies)
  (disjoint : ∀ family ∈ innerFamilies, family ∉ outerFamilies) (inner : TDN.Targets.Component) :
  inner ∈ components →
    ∀ outer ∈ components,
      inner.role = TDN.Targets.ComponentRole.inner →
        outer.role = TDN.Targets.ComponentRole.outer → inner.osFamily ≠ outer.osFamily
TDN.Targets.outer_firewall_placement_from_local_checks {Node Side : Type} {untrusted publicInternet grayRequired : Bool}
  {edge grayConnection : Node → Node → Prop} {outer firewall black grayFirewall inner : Node → Prop}
  {inside outside external : Node → Node → Prop} (side : Node → Side)
  (internetUntrusted : publicInternet = true → untrusted = true)
  (connections : ∀ (a b : Node), inside a b ∨ outside a b ∨ external a b → Relation.ReflTransGen edge a b)
  (placement :
    if publicInternet = true then ∀ (o : Node), outer o → ∃ f b, firewall f ∧ black b ∧ inside o f ∧ outside f b
    else ∀ (o : Node), outer o → ∃ b, black b ∧ external o b)
  (grayPlacement :
    if grayRequired = true then ∀ (o : Node), outer o → ∃ g, grayFirewall g ∧ grayConnection o g
    else ∀ (o : Node), outer o → ∃ i, inner i ∧ grayConnection o i)
  (edgesChecked : untrusted = true → ∀ (a b : Node), edge a b → ¬firewall a → ¬firewall b → side a = side b)
  (outerClear : untrusted = true → ∀ (o : Node), outer o → ¬firewall o)
  (differentSides : untrusted = true → ∀ (o b : Node), outer o → black b → side o ≠ side b) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_outer_firewall_placement untrusted publicInternet grayRequired edge grayConnection outer firewall
    black grayFirewall inner inside outside external
TDN.Targets.outer_gray_policy_from_local_rules {P I : Type} (policy : TDN.Targets.GrayPolicy P I)
  (safe :
    ∀ interface ∈ policy.interfaces,
      ∀ rule ∈ policy.rules interface,
        rule.verdict = TDN.Filter.Verdict.allow →
          ∀ (packet : P),
            rule.accepts packet = true →
              TDN.Targets.GrayCommonPermitted policy interface packet ∧
                (policy.management packet →
                  policy.initiatedByGrayAW packet ∨ policy.outerLoggingOrAuthentication packet))
  (complete :
    ∀ interface ∈ policy.interfaces,
      ∀ (packet : P),
        policy.requiredTraffic interface packet → TDN.Targets.FirstAllowWitness (policy.rules interface) packet) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_outer_gray_interface_policy policy

      ∀ process ∈ inventory outer, TDN.Targets.ObservedNetworkService.routingProtocol ∉ process.services) :
  TDN.Targets.outer_routing_protocols_disabled outers inventory
TDN.Targets.outer_tunnel_requirements_from_local_checks (c : TDN.Execution.Config) (layout : ℕ → List ℕ)
  (inside outside : List ℕ) (component : ℕ) (conditions : TDN.Execution.LocalStackConditions c layout)
  (inboundTags : ∀ (port : ℕ), c.owner port = component → ∀ a ∈ c.inbound port, a.tag = 2)
  (insideCheck : ∀ port ∈ inside, 2 ∉ layout port ∧ 1 ∈ layout port) (outsideCheck : ∀ port ∈ outside, 2 ∈ layout port)
  (components : List TDN.Targets.EncryptionComponentConfiguration) (outboundMACObjective inboundMACObjective : Bool)
  (settings :
    ∀ item ∈ components,
      (item.mechanism = TDN.Mechanism.ipsec → item.espTunnelMode = true) ∧
        (outboundMACObjective = true → item.outboundMACPolicy = true) ∧
          (inboundMACObjective = true → item.inboundMACPolicy = true)) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_outer_tunnel_no_bypass (TDN.Targets.operationalTunnelBoundary c layout inside outside 2 component)
    components outboundMACObjective inboundMACObjective
    (TDN.Targets.operationalTunnelBoundary c layout inside outside 2 component).outboundEntry fun state =>
    1 ∈ state.frame.tags

  (encodingBound : ∀ (bytes : ℕ), sizing.encodedSize bytes ≤ bytes + sizing.encapsulationOverheadBound)
  (outerEncodingBound : ∀ (bytes : ℕ), sizing.outerEncodedSize bytes ≤ bytes + sizing.encapsulationOverheadBound)

  (outer : sizing.payloadLimit + sizing.encapsulationOverheadBound ≤ sizing.outerMtu)
  (black :

      sizing.payloadLimit + sizing.encapsulationOverheadBound ≤ sizing.blackMtu) :
  TDN.Targets.FitsSelectedPath sizing

  (blackEncoding : Monotone sizing.blackEncodedSize)

  (blackCheck : ∀ port ∈ blackPorts, layout port = [2, 1])

  (run : (TDN.Execution.lts c).MTr start actions finish) (ready : finish.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.ready)

  (c : TDN.Execution.Config) (sources : List ℕ) (reception : TDN.Targets.msc_same_domain_plaintext_reception c sources)
  (recipientPort recipientDomain : ℕ) (recipient : c.recipientDomain recipientPort = some recipientDomain)
  {actions : List TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.Action}
  {pieces : List (TDN.Fragmentation.Fragment Header (TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.ByteOrigin Byte ℕ ℕ))} {header : Header}
  {result : TDN.Fragmentation.Datagram Header (TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.ByteOrigin Byte ℕ ℕ)}
  (run : (TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.system (TDN.Targets.ArrivedFragment c sources recipientPort)).MTr [] actions pieces)
  (accepted : TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.assemble header pieces = some result)
  (byte : TDN.ReassemblyOrigins.ByteOrigin Byte ℕ ℕ) : byte ∈ result.bytes → byte.domain = recipientDomain
TDN.Targets.received_domain_from_local_checks (c : TDN.Execution.Config) (layout : ℕ → List ℕ) (allowed : ℕ → Prop)
  (sources : List ℕ) (domain : ℕ) (conditions : TDN.Execution.LocalDomainConditions c layout allowed)
  (sourceCheck :
    ∀ port ∈ sources, c.recipientDomain port = some domain → layout port = [] ∧ c.payloadOrigin port ∧ allowed port)
  (recipientCheck : ∀ (port : ℕ), allowed port → c.recipientDomain port = none ∨ c.recipientDomain port = some domain)
  {start finish : TDN.Execution.State} {actions : List TDN.Execution.Action} {recipient : ℕ}
  (origin : TDN.Targets.RedPayloadOrigin c sources start) (domainAtSource : c.recipientDomain start.port = some domain)
  (run : (TDN.Execution.lts c).MTr start actions finish) (received : finish.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.received)
  (recipientAtEnd : c.recipientDomain finish.port = some recipient) : domain = recipient
TDN.Targets.red_egress_crosses_inner_from_attachment_cut {Node : Type} (edge : Node → Node → Prop) (inner : Node → Prop)
  (red : Node → Bool) (checked : ∀ (a b : Node), edge a b → ¬inner a → ¬inner b → red a = red b)
  {source destination : Node} (start : ¬inner source) (sourceRed : red source = true)
  (destinationOutside : red destination = false) :
  ¬Relation.ReflTransGen (TDN.Paths.Avoiding edge inner) source destination

TDN.Targets.retention_from_enforced_deletion_guard (now : ℕ) (logs : List TDN.Targets.AuditLogRecord)
  (guard : ∀ log ∈ logs, ∀ (time : ℕ), log.deletedAt = some time → log.oneYearAnniversary ≤ time)
  (recorded : ∀ log ∈ logs, ∀ (time : ℕ), log.deletedAt = some time → time ≤ now) (log : TDN.Targets.AuditLogRecord) :
  log ∈ logs → log.createdAt ≤ now → now < log.oneYearAnniversary → log.deletedAt = none
TDN.Targets.revocation_matrix_covers_all_components (compromises components : List ℕ)
  (update : ℕ → ℕ → TDN.Targets.RevocationUpdate)
  (identity :
    ∀ compromise ∈ compromises,
      ∀ component ∈ components,
        (update compromise component).compromise = compromise ∧ (update compromise component).component = component)
  (compromise : ℕ) :
  compromise ∈ compromises →
    ∀ component ∈ components,
      ∃ record ∈ List.flatMap (fun c => List.map (update c) components) compromises,
        record.compromise = compromise ∧ record.component = component

  (origin : TDN.Targets.RedPayloadOrigin c sources start)
  (sourceLevel : c.recipientDomain start.port = some sourceDomain)
  (run :
    (TDN.KernelResponses.lts (TDN.Execution.lts c) (TDN.Targets.nativeKernelResponses c gateways)).MTr
      (TDN.KernelResponses.State.network start) actions (TDN.KernelResponses.State.network finish))
  (delivered : finish.phase = TDN.Execution.Phase.delivered)
  (recipientLevel : c.recipientDomain finish.port = some recipientDomain) : sourceDomain = recipientDomain
TDN.Targets.security_connectivity_from_delivery_and_peer_checks {S A : Type}
  (view : TDN.Targets.PacketExecutionView S A) (networks : List TDN.RedNetworkIdentity)
  (authenticatedInnerPairs requiredConnectivity : List (String × String)) (innerLevel : String → TDN.SecurityLevel)
  (dedicated : TDN.DedicatedInnerAssignments networks)
  (peerChecks : ∀ pair ∈ authenticatedInnerPairs, innerLevel pair.1 = innerLevel pair.2)
  (deliveryCheck :
    ∀ (start finish : S) (actions : List A),
      view.source start →
        view.system.MTr start actions finish →
          view.plaintextDelivered finish →
            ∃ level,
              TDN.Targets.NetworkHasLevel networks (view.network start) level ∧
                TDN.Targets.NetworkHasLevel networks (view.network finish) level)
  (positive : ∀ pair ∈ requiredConnectivity, TDN.Targets.ModelDelivers view pair.1 pair.2) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_security_level_connectivity view networks authenticatedInnerPairs requiredConnectivity innerLevel

  (peers : List TDN.Targets.InnerAuthentication) (authorityLevel keyLevel : String → TDN.SecurityLevel)
  (scopeFacts : ∀ peer ∈ peers, TDN.Targets.CredentialScoped authorityLevel keyLevel peer)
  (present : managementSelected = true → ∀ peer ∈ peers, peer.managementDevices.Nonempty)
  (management :
    managementSelected = true →
      ∀ a ∈ peers, ∀ b ∈ peers, a.level ≠ b.level → Disjoint a.managementDevices b.managementDevices) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_security_level_key_and_management_separation managementSelected peers

TDN.Targets.transec_conditions_from_cover_installations (installations : List TDN.Targets.CoverInstallation)
  (binding : ∀ i ∈ installations, i.use.enabled = i.active)
  (checked :
    ∀ i ∈ installations,
      i.use.publicInternet = true ∨
          i.use.objectiveSelected = true ∧ i.use.externalMACsec = true ∧ i.use.outerFirewallAttached = true →
        i.active = true ∧ i.sender.Valid) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_transec_conditions (List.map TDN.Targets.CoverInstallation.use installations) ∧
    ∀ i ∈ installations,
      i.use.publicInternet = true ∨
          i.use.objectiveSelected = true ∧ i.use.externalMACsec = true ∧ i.use.outerFirewallAttached = true →
        TDN.Targets.ScheduledCoverGuarantee i.sender
TDN.Targets.trusted_time_from_component_assignments {C S : Type} (model : TDN.Targets.TimeSynchronizationModel C S)
  (assigned : C → S)
  (red :
    ∀ c ∈ model.components,
      model.inner c ∨ model.redManagementService c → model.locatedIn (assigned c) TDN.Targets.TimeNetwork.red)
  (gray :
    ∀ c ∈ model.components,
      model.outer c ∨ model.grayManagementService c ∨ model.grayFirewall c →
        model.locatedIn (assigned c) TDN.Targets.TimeNetwork.grayManagement)
  (trust :
    ∀ c ∈ model.components,
      model.inner c ∨
          model.redManagementService c ∨ model.outer c ∨ model.grayManagementService c ∨ model.grayFirewall c →
        model.trusted (assigned c))
  (synchronized :
    ∀ c ∈ model.components,
      model.inner c ∨
          model.redManagementService c ∨ model.outer c ∨ model.grayManagementService c ∨ model.grayFirewall c →
        model.synchronizedTo c (assigned c)) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_trusted_time_synchronization model

  (checked : List.filter (fun interface => interface.enabled) unused = [])
  (interface : TDN.Targets.UnusedInterfaceState) : interface ∈ unused → interface.enabled = false
TDN.Targets.version_deadlines_of_earlier_limit (change : TDN.Targets.VersionChange)
  (notified : change.publishedAt ≤ change.notifiedAt)
  (calendar : change.notificationDeadline = TDN.CalendarTime.sixMonthsAfter change.notifiedAt)
  (publication : change.publishedAt ≤ change.compliantAt)
  (earlier : change.compliantAt ≤ min change.nextRenewal change.notificationDeadline)
  (registration :
    change.compliantAt ≤ change.reregistration.time ∧ change.reregistration.time ≤ change.notificationDeadline)
  (identity : change.reregistration.solution = change.solution ∧ change.reregistration.cpVersion = change.cpVersion) :
  TDN.Targets.VersionDeadlines change
TDN.Targets.vpn_mode_policies_from_complete_local_checks (gateways : List TDN.Targets.VPNModePolicy)
  (versions : ∀ g ∈ gateways, g.ikeVersions ≠ [] ∧ ∀ version ∈ g.ikeVersions, version = 2)
  (ike :
    ∀ g ∈ gateways,
      g.ikeModes ≠ [] ∧ ∀ mode ∈ g.ikeModes, mode = TDN.Targets.CipherMode.cbc ∨ mode = TDN.Targets.CipherMode.gcm)
  (esp :
    ∀ g ∈ gateways,
      g.espProposals ≠ [] ∧
        ∀ proposal ∈ g.espProposals,
          proposal.mode = TDN.Targets.CipherMode.cbc ∧ proposal.hmacIntegrity = true ∨
            proposal.mode = TDN.Targets.CipherMode.gcm) :
  TDN.Targets.msc_ike_version_and_encryption_modes gateways

      (TDN.KernelResponses.State.network before) actions (TDN.KernelResponses.State.network after))

TDN.black_has_two_layers {n : TDN.Network} {a b : TDN.State} (h : Relation.ReflTransGen (TDN.Step n) a b)
  (ha : TDN.WellLayered a) (black : b.zone = TDN.Zone.black) :
  ∃ o i, b.seals = [o, i] ∧ o.layer = TDN.Layer.outer ∧ i.layer = TDN.Layer.inner
TDN.concrete_black_protection {Concrete : Type} {concreteStep : Concrete → Concrete → Prop}
  {abstract : Concrete → TDN.State} {n : TDN.Network} (refinement : TDN.Refines concreteStep abstract n)
  {a b : Concrete} (h : Relation.ReflTransGen concreteStep a b) (initial : TDN.WellLayered (abstract a))
  (black : (abstract b).zone = TDN.Zone.black) :
  ∃ o i, (abstract b).seals = [o, i] ∧ o.layer = TDN.Layer.outer ∧ i.layer = TDN.Layer.inner
TDN.execution_preserves_level {n : TDN.Network} {a b : TDN.State} (h : Relation.ReflTransGen (TDN.Step n) a b) :
  b.level = a.level
TDN.inner_delivery_same_level {n : TDN.Network} {a b : TDN.State} (h : TDN.Step n a b)
  (delivered : b.zone = TDN.Zone.red) : n.innerLevel b.location = a.level
